Hash ≠ authenticity/custody proof.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Genuine interview/document/photo comparison; no raw-video interpretation or irrelevant photo garnish.\\r\\n17. **Capability fit — INFERENCE:** Per-file document QA/extract/reconcile, single-photo OCR/VLM/meta, audio transcription, evidence collection, saved text [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 + audio format/segment-location reliability = **7 groups**. Unsupported: raw-video extraction, NLE integration, custom index, scheduler, rights adjudication.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** SourceWeave helps documentary fact-checkers review assertions against selected interviews, archive images and documents already on a NAS. It returns support, contradictions and missing primary evidence with real source pointers for human approval. It does not edit video, certify truth or clear rights. The first device test must prove source-location fidelity and abstention.\\r\\n20. **One-day device feasibility — INFERENCE:** CONDITIONAL, 12–20 synthetic files, one short interview, two script revisions. Fail if pointers/extraction fail; no hidden preprocessing.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** 0:00–0:30 “opened in 1972”; 0:30–1:30 interview versus dated 1974 source; 1:30–2:20 actual pointers plus unsupported causal assertion; 2:20–3:00 human leaves unresolved and saves. Not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS feature collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH for search/OCR/summary, MEDIUM for full assertion-review job [S03–S05].\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** MEDIUM, with substantial Trint/Axle vertical overlap.\\r\\n24. **Generic LLM suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy after documentary context; evidence labels do not create a moat.\\r\\n25. **Judge objection — INFERENCE:** “Trint plus a prompt; Axle already runs locally.”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Documented costly post-search job and credible media-data gravity; conflict is legible to judges.\\r\\n27. **Kill gate — INFERENCE:** Practitioners already do this smoothly in their MAM, pointers are fabricated, or same-input generic AI has no meaningful workflow disadvantage.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W02 — LeadTrace: investigative prepublication dossier\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Investigative journalist [S40–S41].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Prepare allegation-to-evidence review distinguishing ambiguous identities and missing support.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Documents, scans and extracted entities/connections [S40–S41].\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — FACT:** Datashare supports self-hosted/local analysis [S40]. INFERENCE: N2, not UGREEN demand.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Datashare/Aleph extraction, search and investigation structures [S40–S41].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Same-name entities or inference chains become unsupported allegations.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Rework, editorial delay and reputational/identity harm; no legal outcome predicted.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Datashare and Aleph [S40–S41].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** OCR/extraction/search, entities and investigative relationships/timelines [S40–S41].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Final cross-export assertion ledger; no proven gap in these strong tools.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Strong same-input models can check claims; a private-RAG wrapper is insufficient.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Local history fits storage, but specialist self-hosted tools may be a better fit.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Passage support, entity ambiguity and chronology, never autonomous accusation.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Editor/reporter decides identity, truth, source protection and publication.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central; actual passages and unresolved ambiguity.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Optional for the central text dossier; do not force it.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Document QA/extract/reconcile/save; no graph service or web investigation assumed [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 = **6 groups**. Unsupported: registry enrichment, graph backend, ACL enforcement.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** LeadTrace would prepare an allegation checklist from selected local investigation records, preserving direct passages, unresolved identities and contradictions. It would neither accuse people nor publish. Datashare and Aleph already solve much of the job; a credible entry first needs evidence of an unmet cross-export review step.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** Tiny synthetic dossier feasible conditionally; no validation of newsroom adoption/confidentiality.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** Two same-name entities and unsupported allegation; abstention shown. Not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH private document RAG.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH evidence binder.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy with journalist context.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “Why not Datashare/Aleph and the editorial checklist?”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Serious error cost and established self-hosting.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Reject from Top 6 without evidence of a remaining specialist-tool handoff gap.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W03 — ConditionReturn: museum loan-return reconciliation\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Museum registrar with conservator [S15–S17].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Reconcile returned object identity, outgoing/return reports and required image views before accepting the record.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Loan/object IDs, condition notes and photographs [S15–S17].\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — FACT:** Museum NAS archive exists [S14]. INFERENCE: N3 adjacent sector, only N1 for loan records; large film archives do not prove small-museum report storage.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Review previous condition, identify records, photograph/check condition and document changes [S15–S17].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Wrong loan/object, missing view or unexplained change hides behind a complete-looking folder.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Return clarification, conservation triage and disputed responsibility; no damage/liability diagnosis.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Articheck and Axiell EMu [S18–S19].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Multimedia condition history/annotations in Articheck; conservation/condition history with multimedia in EMu [S18–S19].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Historic nonstandard exports against a current loan checklist, outside integrated collections tools.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Same-input models can compare records. Need better NAS scope/identity/source continuity, not a smarter damage description.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Existing originals and review artifact share persistent local storage; actual local custody must be established.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** IDs, dates, stated-condition disagreements and user-required view coverage; visual observation is a hypothesis.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Conservator decides damage/change; registrar approves acceptance, loan status and communications.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central; specific object, source and observation, with unresolved mappings.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Necessary for photo/report/object-view correspondence; not calibrated pixel-level comparison.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Single-photo OCR/VLM/meta, document extract/compare/reconcile, saved text [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 + object/view mapping = **7 groups**. Unsupported: annotation renderer, measured damage detection, collection-system integration or automatic claims.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** ConditionReturn helps a registrar check a returned museum loan against outgoing records. It lists object-ID conflicts, missing agreed photo views and condition statements needing a conservator, with actual source pointers. It does not diagnose damage or assign liability. Its value depends on records retained outside an already integrated collections system.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** CONDITIONAL, 10–16 synthetic reports/photos, one object plus decoy loan; prove ID and view mapping first.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** 0:00–0:30 loan handoff; 0:30–1:30 similar decoy object and missing reverse view; 1:30–2:20 “unchanged” statement conflicts with observation; 2:20–3:00 conservator escalation with pointers. Not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH search/image description; MEDIUM full loan-review job.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** MEDIUM; inspection is a predictable extension, with mature vertical products.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Less obvious unseeded, easy from museum context; not LOW.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “Articheck already does this; VLM cannot assess damage.”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Documented professional boundary and vivid identity/completeness failures without physical intervention.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Existing Articheck/EMu already resolves target handoff, source/object mapping fails, or users require automatic damage diagnosis.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W04 — ConsentBoundary: oral-history deposit preflight\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Oral-history archivist / accession coordinator [S20–S22].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Reconcile interview identity, release versions, narrator review and restrictions before deposit.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Recordings, transcripts, release forms, contextual records and restrictions [S20–S22].\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — FACT:** OHA archive manual explicitly discusses shared-server/cloud workflows [S21]. INFERENCE: N2, not NAS-brand prevalence.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Preserve consent/context alongside media; institutions define specific use permissions [S20–S22], not universal law.\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Findable audio has absent, superseded or wrong-interview authorization.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Deposit delay, restricted reuse and unwanted exposure; not automated legal compliance.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** OHMS and Aviary [S23–S24], integrated adjacent tools, not independent vendors.\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Metadata/indexing/synchronization and searchable media presentation/access controls [S23–S24].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Legacy local release/restriction reconciliation before platform ingest.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Same files suffice for generic reasoning; repeatable pre-deposit review over local originals must add actual value.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Review long-lived audio and consent files before publishing-system ingest; network locality still UNKNOWN.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Interview/date correspondence, incompatible restrictions, absent review evidence and superseded forms; not consent inferred from silence.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Archivist/rights holder interprets consent and approves access/deposit. Never an ACL/publication agent.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central; release version and real passage/segment locations.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Necessary when spoken restriction conflicts with written form; otherwise documents may suffice. ASR alone establishes no consent.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Per-file document review, audio transcription, evidence collection and saved text [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 + ASR restriction/speaker localization + release-version mapping = **8 groups**. Unsupported: ACL, publishing, e-signatures, narrator notifications.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** ConsentBoundary helps oral-history archivists prepare a deposit without confusing searchable media with permission to use it. It checks selected interview files, releases and restriction notes, reporting conflicts and missing evidence with source pointers. An archivist resolves every access decision. It neither changes permissions nor publishes material.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** CONDITIONAL, 10–18 synthetic files, two releases and two short audio excerpts. If restriction attribution fails, the audio-dependent concept fails; document-only reduction is separate.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** 0:00–0:30 deposit; 0:30–1:30 later embargo versus earlier release; 1:30–2:20 missing release for another recording; 2:20–3:00 unresolved archivist review, no permission changes. Not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH retrieval/transcription; MEDIUM pre-deposit restriction reconciliation.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** MEDIUM; consent/compliance assistants remain predictable.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy with oral-history context, not novel by name.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “You cannot enforce consent; Aviary already has permissions.”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Pre-deposit consistency is distinct from enforcement; explicit shared-server practice and professional guidance.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Value requires enforcement/publishing, precedence is unavailable, or ASR fabricates attribution/locations.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W05 — CaseBinder—TradeCloseout: installed-equipment handoff\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Trade subcontractor closeout coordinator [S25–S26].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Check one installed-equipment package against owner-approved requirements before handover.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Manuals, warranties, submittals and change records [S25–S26]. ASSUMPTION: Plate photos/readable exports are available.\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — INFERENCE:** N1: contractor project exports plausibly use file shares; sources do not establish NAS prevalence. All-Procore firms may lack this need.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Contract-specific binders and changes are reconciled using project workflows [S25–S27].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Every document type exists but the manual/model, warranty or revision is wrong for the installed asset.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Resubmission, handover delay and maintenance confusion; no invented payment-delay figure.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Procore and Autodesk Pype / Forma Operations [S25,S27].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Pype extracts requirements, reminds, collects/packages and handles specification versions; Procore offers submittal/action-plan workflows [S25,S27].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Installed-ID discrepancies across supplier exports, plate photos and changed requirements; not evidence Pype cannot do it.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** A generic model with same packet/checklist can reason similarly; local repeatability and reliable coverage must justify use.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Only material for contractors already retaining originals on NAS; image/document review without moving a full archive.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Required item→installed asset→matching approved document; contradictions and missing support, not engineering adequacy.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Trade lead/owner controls requirements, revision precedence and acceptance; no payment release/certification.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central; requirement and actual installed-ID support for each exception.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Plate-photo versus document-ID is genuine; PDF-only completeness is a weaker crowded reduction.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Photo OCR/meta, per-file document QA/reconcile and evidence text output [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 + equipment-ID normalization = **7 groups**. Unsupported: BIM/CAD, email/reminders, PM/ERP APIs, automatic acceptance.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** CaseBinder TradeCloseout checks a selected equipment handoff against an approved checklist. It connects installed nameplate evidence to manuals, warranties and approved revisions, listing mismatches and missing support for human review. It is not project management. Pype and Procore already automate closeout; cross-export discrepancy handling must earn its place.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** CONDITIONAL, 12–24 synthetic files/two assets, wrong-model manual and changed checklist; prove OCR and text path first.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** 0:00–0:30 apparently complete folder; 0:30–1:30 wrong model; 1:30–2:20 obsolete revision and absent training evidence; 2:20–3:00 human review artifact. Not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH summary/checklist substeps; MEDIUM full installed-ID reconciliation.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH completeness/compliance binder, compounded by mature vertical automation.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy; trade label and provenance do not lower collision.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “Pype already automates closeout; why NAS?”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Concrete deliverable, objective mismatches and relatively simple bounded inputs.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Work already fully in Procore/Pype, originals incidental to NAS, or BIM/integration necessary for value.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W06 — DesignDelta: drawing/RFI revision review\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Architect/design coordinator comparing drawings [S28].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Determine which issued changes affect unresolved RFIs before release.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** PDF drawings/markups [S28]. ASSUMPTION: RFI text/issue registers accompany them.\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — INFERENCE:** N1 shared project sets plausible, prevalence unverified.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Bluebeam Compare/Overlay highlights drawing changes [S28].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Geometric change contradicts an old approval while filenames appear current.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Re-review/coordination error; no quantified construction loss.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Bluebeam Revu; adjacent Autodesk construction review [S26,S28].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Graphical comparisons/overlays and project review workflows [S26,S28].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Geometric change→RFI meaning still needs specialist review; no new market gap established.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Generic multimodal models may help; text-only comparisons are not equivalent to geometry.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Storage cannot supply the missing core operation.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Geometry/obligation relationship, not filename or text revision alone.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Design professionals approve meaning/issue status.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central drawing revision/location and approval record.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Essential geometric/visual task, not satisfied by OCR.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Registers may be readable; drawing overlay/render/geometry is not established [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — FACT (catalog-bounded): HARD_BLOCKER** for intended geometric comparison. UNKNOWN G1–G6; no custom CAD engine.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** DesignDelta would reconcile issued drawing changes with unresolved RFIs. Reliable geometric comparison is central, and the public Skill catalog does not expose it. A text-only revision checklist would be a different reduced topic. The full workflow should not advance by hiding that limitation.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** Not feasible as defined; no sanctioned geometry operation established.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** An overlay would imply unsupported functionality; no feasible full-scope demo asserted or built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH text/revision summary; geometry is a separate specialist market.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH design-document assistant.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy revision-assistant idea.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “Where is the actual drawing comparison?”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Real consequential review problem, outweighed by blocker.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Reject original scope absent sanctioned geometry capability; text diff is not a substitute.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W07 — ClaimPacket: property-loss evidence preparation\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Policyholder/restoration documentation coordinator [S42–S43].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Link item claims to photos/inventory/receipts for human review.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Inventory, claim records, field photos/notes/reports [S42–S43].\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — INFERENCE:** N1 weak; phone/cloud capture may dominate, no essential NAS proven.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Maintain records; Encircle aggregates field documentation/reports [S42–S43].\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Duplicate identities, absent receipt or inconsistent dates.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Clarification/resubmission; no coverage/settlement prediction.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** Encircle; adjacent general multimodal AI [S43,S06–S08].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** Field photos/video/notes, carrier-oriented reports and AI features [S43].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Old private records outside field tools; weak evidence of NAS-native gap.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Same small packet easily uploaded to a strong multimodal model; no exclusive reasoning advantage.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Conditional on substantial pre-loss local history; otherwise a generic binder is simpler.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Item/date consistency and support, never causation, value or entitlement.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Claimant/professional decides content; insurer decides coverage/submission outcome.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central receipt/item/photo correspondence.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Genuine photos/receipts, already served by restoration tools.\\r\\n17. **Fit — INFERENCE:** Photo OCR/VLM and document reconciliation for tiny precheck [P04].\\r\\n18. **Dependencies — UNKNOWN:** G1–G6 = **6 groups**. Unsupported: insurer API, valuation engine or auto-submission.\\r\\n19. **Round 1 explanation:** ClaimPacket would check selected loss files for item mismatches and missing evidence. It would not assess coverage or value. Existing restoration tools already generate sophisticated reports, and a NAS-specific advantage is weakly evidenced. It is a negative comparison, not a shortlisted direction.\\r\\n20. **Device day — INFERENCE:** Tiny synthetic packet conditionally feasible; customer value unvalidated.\\r\\n21. **Three-minute concept — INFERENCE:** Similar items and wrong receipt; understandable but familiar, not built.\\r\\n22. **NAS collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH photo/document extraction.\\r\\n23. **Hackathon collision — INFERENCE:** HIGH evidence binder.\\r\\n24. **Generic suggestion — INFERENCE:** Easy.\\r\\n25. **Objection — INFERENCE:** “Encircle plus uploads already does the work.”\\r\\n26. **Keep reason — INFERENCE:** Clear planted error and accessible synthetic inputs.\\r\\n27. **Kill — INFERENCE:** Reject without a persistent NAS-native professional gap beyond current restoration systems.\\r\\n\\r\\n### W08 — SampleTrail: field-sample handoff reconciliation\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **User — FACT:** Environmental project lead / lab sample custodian [S29–S30].\\r\\n2. **Job — INFERENCE:** Reconcile approved plan, field IDs, labels, custody amendments and lab receipt before human handoff acceptance.\\r\\n3. **Data — FACT:** Plans, collection records, labels and custody/evidence forms [S29–S30]. ASSUMPTION: Readable exports and label photos authorized.\\r\\n4. **NAS gravity — FACT:** EPA branch SOP describes project-linked forms/logbooks on LAN [S30]. INFERENCE: N2, not all-lab NAS prevalence or UGREEN qualification.\\r\\n5. **Today — FACT:** Plan-guided collection and traceable record corrections/evidence handling [S29–S30]. Branch SOP is not universal law.\\r\\n6. **Failure — INFERENCE:** Aliases, superseded custody and label mismatch survive file-presence checks; legitimate QA duplicates get merged incorrectly.\\r\\n7. **Cost — INFERENCE:** Clarification, delayed acceptance and questioned interpretability; missing file ≠ physically lost sample.\\r\\n8. **Tools — FACT:** ESdat Field Programs/LSPECS and EarthSoft EQuIS field tools [S31–S34].\\r\\n9. **Solved — FACT:** ESdat includes offline collection, photos/eCoC and expected-value/sample-plan-to-lab checks [S31]. EDGE docs cover field/custody workflow [S33]; detailed current Collect behavior not fully retrieved [S34].\\r\\n10. **Awkward step — ASSUMPTION:** Legacy PDF/photo/export islands outside a unified field/LIMS system. ESdat may already remove the gap.\\r\\n11. **Generic AI test — INFERENCE:** Same-input model can reconcile IDs; persistence, coverage and abstention must improve the actual handoff, not claim special intelligence.\\r\\n12. **UGREEN relevance — INFERENCE:** Review existing shared project history and label images without replacing field collection or LIMS.\\r\\n13. **Reasoning — INFERENCE:** Plan→field→label→custody→lab alias, dated amendments and intentionally distinct QA samples; not analytical-result or safety judgment.\\r\\n14. **Human approval — INFERENCE:** Lead/custodian resolves aliases, signs corrections and accepts samples. Never fabricated custody signature.\\r\\n15. **Provenance — INFERENCE:** Central; each link needs actual record/location and explicit scope.\\r\\n16. **Multimodal — INFERENCE:** Label-photo versus text-record reconciliation is necessary; plain custody documents face strong incumbent overlap.\\r\\n\\r\\n| # | 風險 | 嚴重度 | 依據 |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| R1 | **決賽前無法實機驗證**：只有靜態 checker；所有 action | FACT S1 |\\r\\n| R12 | 英文要求（提交、影片旁白或字幕） | 低 | FACT S1 S2 |\\r\\n| V1 | 列出散落副本及時間／file-agent | `ug-filesystem find --path S --type documents`；`stat --path P` | read | none；核准 scope | SUPPORTED | 可比較內容／document-agent | `ug-document qa --query Q --file P --intent content_read --content-scope full` | read | none | UNKNOWN | 特定格式、掃描頁、body-cap/分頁是否完整未給契約；未取得內容不得生成 diff。未證實 |\\r\\n| V4 | This finding does not claim measured accuracy or equal performance; those remain UNKNOWN.\\r\\n\\r\\nThree follow-up comparison arms are **defined, not run**:\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **Bounded-file arm:** All models receive the identical small packet and strong instructions: “List every supplied source and read status; quote exact evidence; distinguish conflict, absent support and unreadable records; apply only the given version policy; do not infer approval; save the requested table if your environment permits.” Include setup/prompting effort for every arm, not just competitors. Content parity is expected, not an embarrassment to hide.\\r\\n2. **Persistent-workspace arm:** Compare against a general AI with authorized mounted/local/connected files and file output, not only a web upload chat. Repeat a manually initiated review after replacing/adding a source; give both systems the same updated scope. RSpace/Axle/Nextcloud/local RAG are additional strong controls where relevant.\\r\\n3. **Existing-vertical-tool arm:** Use the user's already configured tool with normal templates/exports, not a deliberately unconfigured trial. Include a Procore/Pype workflow for B, Trint/Axle/Datashare for A and RSpace/eLabFTW/OMERO for C. Account for integration setup fairly; no assertion that our Skill is cheaper/easier without measurement.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Potential remaining value — ASSUMPTION:** A repeatable packet-review contract at the current NAS location could reduce repeated record selection, attachment/version confusion and rechecking, while saving the exact input coverage and reviewer disposition beside originals. **It is neither unique to NAS nor demonstrated here.** A general file agent can implement it. If the only difference is a nicer table, a prewritten prompt or saving a text file, the horizontal competition positioning should be dropped rather than called a new evidence engine.\\r\\n\\r\\n### The three strongest objections, answered directly\\r\\n\\r\\n**1. “把同一批檔案丟給 ChatGPT 不就可以了？” — INFERENCE:** For this small, authorized packet, **yes, probably**. The proposed benefit is not a superior answer. It would have to be less preparation/rechecking over recurring existing NAS records, with equally reliable source locations and coverage. ChatGPT local-file workspaces and connected context narrow even that benefit [R13]. We have not demonstrated that it remains. Claiming upload friction while comparing only a tiny static packet would be invalid.\\r\\n\\r\\n**2. “這不就是 RAG 加 supporting / contradicting 標籤？” — INFERENCE:** The original framing largely is. RAG can support multi-source reasoning, and an agent around it can check requirements and persist a review ledger. The revised contract adds accountable input/read/failure scope and version-linked human disposition; these are workflow requirements, **not** a novel algorithm, accuracy guarantee or barrier to a capable RAG developer. It survives only if that contract improves an actual handoff over strong baselines. Retrieval failure must never be relabeled “missing evidence.”\\r\\n\\r\\n**3. “為什麼一定需要 NAS，而不是 SaaS？” — INFERENCE:** It does not categorically require NAS. SaaS with connectors, or an on-prem vertical system, can provide the same review. NAS is materially relevant for a team already keeping authoritative mixed originals there and repeatedly reviewing them without another maintained copy. Current public engineering evidence has not established that target cohort. Optional cloud models also invalidate unconditional privacy claims [R03]. If a SaaS/file-aware alternative produces the same outcome with equal preparation and trustworthy continuity, no NAS-specific advantage remains.\\r\\n\\r\\n## D. Competitor landscape\\r\\n\\r\\nFACT rows summarize documented/advertised functions only. “Not clearly solved” is explicitly UNKNOWN unless stated otherwise. No exhaustive product evaluation, installation, feature absence or superiority claim is implied. SourceWeave / Documentary-Investigative\\r\\n\\r\\n**FACT:** Documentary checking uses assertion/primary-source/page/quote/checker records [R17]. A real film company stores long-lived media and production documentation on NAS [R19]; this is sector evidence, not proof its fact-checking runs there. **INFERENCE:** Test one script review across documents, archive stills and authorized interview transcripts/audio. Investigative entity claims add Datashare/Aleph pressure and sensitivity; do not silently expand the documentary test to automated investigations.\\r\\n\\r\\n**ASSUMPTION:** Cross-folder checker preparation outside Trint/Axle remains costly. Genuine multimodal value occurs when an image/date record and interview account qualify a written assertion; adding an unrelated photo does not count. Human decides editorial truth, source quality and clearance. No video editing, automated journalism, rights clearance or final factual verdict.\\r\\n\\r\\n### B. Engineering / Project Evidence Review\\r\\n\\r\\n**FACT:** Closeout requires organized contract-specific delivery records; Procore exposes approved/returned submittal responses and revision history, while Autodesk describes closeout verification and substantial Pype automation [R22–R26]. **INFERENCE:** Test one equipment handoff: compare the supplied approved requirement and revision policy against an installed-label photograph, supplier record and acceptance evidence. This NAS premise is weaker than A/C and is a must-pass gate, not hidden inside a high score. **UNKNOWN:** OCR ambiguity, date/identity mapping, uninspected records and incumbent coverage. A photo of a nameplate does not prove site/time/installation authenticity.\\r\\n\\r\\n### C. Scientist reviews the linkage. This scope can use ordinary readable records/images without certifying the underlying result.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis is **not** the previously rejected full FigureLineage transformation validator. Do not automatically reject C because statistical verification or crop lineage is unavailable; the task explicitly excludes them. Conversely, linking an exported image to a sample ID does not prove the image is the original acquisition or establish reproducibility. **ASSUMPTION:** Correct identity/authority maps and sanctioned readable exports exist. **UNKNOWN:** Native instrument/microscopy formats, domain metadata, version semantics and actual workflow value outside ELN/LIMS. If native format/quantitative verification is necessary for customer value, original capability blockers return and C must stop; a screenshot cannot hide them.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Sixteen required dimensions\\r\\n\\r\\nAll judgments are INFERENCE; confidence in customer-specific gaps remains lower than confidence in job existence.\\r\\n\\r\\n| Dimension | A — Documentary / investigative | B — Engineering / project | C — Research / scientific |\\r\\n|---|---|---|---|\\r\\n| User pain | Manual assertion/source checking documented; urgency varies | Explicit handoff/requirements problem and re-review | Serious linkage integrity; precise user task less directly evidenced |\\r\\n| NAS necessity | Strong adjacent film-NAS case; tiny checker packet may not need raw archive | Weak public proof; IDs alone could use rules | Interpret notes/link context; structured IDs may need only joins |\\r\\n| Multimodal value | Real interview + image + document; transcript-only weakens claim | Nameplate image versus approval/document, visually concrete | Image/record/export identity; raw formats add risk |\\r\\n| Evidence/provenance centrality | Central source/authority dispute | Central asset/revision/record correspondence | Central source/sample relationship; not scientific validity |\\r\\n| Human review | Essential editorial/source judgment | Essential approval/applicability/acceptance | Essential experimental/scientific interpretation |\\r\\n| Market credibility | Established role, unknown willingness to adopt | Established delivery job, no verified NAS cohort | Established infrastructure, new overlay value unknown |\\r\\n| Competition differentiation | Evidence contract more specific than MAM; reproducible by prompting | Concrete “present versus right asset” moment, crowded closeout family | Unusual story, high risk of implying integrity guarantees |\\r\\n| Vertical substitution | Trint/Axle/Datashare/Aleph strong | Procore/Pype strong, more complete than our precheck | OMERO/eLabFTW/RSpace especially strong on governed records |\\r\\n| Generic-AI substitution | HIGH for same packet | HIGH for same packet | HIGH for same packet |\\r\\n| Demo clarity | Memorable but historical claims/authority can need explanation | Strongest: model label versus approved record, plus unread source | Sample-ID mismatch clear, significance needs domain context |\\r\\n| 500-word explainability | Feasible if one assertion;
